FAQ
Questions about product sales
How aftercare and merch fit into the studio workflow.
Can I sell products together with a tattoo appointment? + Yes. Add products in Event Payment and charge the service and products in one mixed checkout. The linked product order and receipt stay visible from the event.
Can I also create a sale without an appointment? + Yes. Standalone product orders remain available for counter sales that are not tied to an event.
How are returns different from cancelling a service payment? + A service payment can follow the event cancellation flow. A sold product is not cancelled directly from Payments; it must go through the product return and refund workflow so stock and finance remain consistent.
Can clients buy studio products on my website now? + Not yet. Website product integration is in development. The current Sales workflow covers the studio catalog, stock, in-studio checkout, orders, receipts and reporting.

AFTERCARE INSTRUCTIONS\nI agree to strictly follow all aftercare instructions provided by the artist, including:\n- Regular cleaning and treatment\n- Avoiding direct sunlight and water exposure during healing\n- Avoiding baths, saunas, pools until fully healed\n- Using recommended aftercare products\n\n6.
